{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01NdVX77EQ1fhTD2DgnUuDar","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01SsAYE8Tr82m7QRkbj26mjP","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"cat > /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v10.sh << 'SCRIPT'\n#!/bin/bash\nset -eo pipefail\n\nsource /etc/network_turbo\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\nexport HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1\nexport HF_ENDPOINT=https://hf-mirror.com\nexport REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\nexport SSL_CERT_FILE=/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\n\nBASEDIR=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft\"\nOUTBASE=\"$BASEDIR/results/exp005_zoo_expansion\"\n\n# Step 1: Re-download HGRN from ModelScope\necho \"[DOWNLOAD] Re-downloading HGRN from ModelScope...\"\nrm -f /root/autodl-tmp/.ms_cache/.lock/m-a-p___340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\npython3 << 'DLEOF'\nfrom modelscope import snapshot_download\nimport shutil, os\n\nmodel_dir = snapshot_download('m-a-p/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/.ms_cache')\nprint('HGRN downloaded to:', model_dir)\n\ndst = '/root/entrainment_gated_ft/models/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline'\nsrc_st = os.path.join(model_dir, 'model.safetensors')\ndst_st = os.path.join(dst, 'model.safetensors')\nshutil.copy2(src_st, dst_st)\nprint(f'Copied {os.path.getsize(dst_st)} bytes -> {dst_st}')\n\n# Verify\nfrom safetensors import safe_open\nf = safe_open(dst_st, framework='pt')\nkeys = list(f.keys())\nprint(f'Verified: {len(keys)} tensors in safetensors')\nDLEOF\necho \"[DOWNLOAD] HGRN download and verification complete\"\n\n# Step 2: Eval function\neval_model() {\n    local NAME=$1\n    local LOCAL=$2\n    local OUTDIR=\"$OUTBASE/${NAME}_pure_baseline\"\n\n    if [ -d \"$OUTDIR\" ] && find \"$OUTDIR\" -name \"results_*.json\" -newer /tmp/v10_start_marker 2>/dev/null | grep -q .; then\n        echo \"[SKIP] $NAME already evaluated at $OUTDIR\"\n        return\n    fi\n\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n    echo \"[EVAL] $NAME from $LOCAL\"\n    echo \"==========================================\"\n    mkdir -p \"$OUTDIR\"\n\n    python3 << PYEOF\nimport fla\n# Fix transformers 5.x compatibility\nimport fla.models.gla.modeling_gla as _gla\nimport fla.models.delta_net.modeling_delta_net as _dn\nimport fla.models.hgrn.modeling_hgrn as _hgrn\nimport fla.models.gated_deltanet.modeling_gated_deltanet as _gdn\n\nfor mod in [_gla, _dn, _hgrn, _gdn]:\n    for attr_name in dir(mod):\n        obj = getattr(mod, attr_name, None)\n        if isinstance(obj, type) and hasattr(obj, '_tied_weights_keys'):\n            if isinstance(obj._tied_weights_keys, list):\n                obj._tied_weights_keys = {'lm_head.weight': 'model.embeddings.weight'}\n\nimport sys\nsys.argv = [\n    'lm_eval',\n    '--model', 'hf',\n    '--model_args', 'pretrained=${LOCAL},trust_remote_code=True,dtype=bfloat16',\n    '--tasks', 'lambada_openai,arc_easy',\n    '--device', 'cuda:0',\n    '--batch_size', 'auto',\n    '--log_samples',\n    '--output_path', '${OUTDIR}/',\n]\nfrom lm_eval.__main__ import cli_evaluate\ncli_evaluate()\nPYEOF\n    echo \"[DONE] $NAME eval complete. Results at $OUTDIR\"\n}\n\ntouch /tmp/v10_start_marker\n\n# GLA already has valid results from v9, skip it\n# But check explicitly\nGLA_RESULT=$(find \"$OUTBASE/GLA_pure_baseline\" -name \"results_*.json\" 2>/dev/null | head -1)\nif [ -n \"$GLA_RESULT\" ]; then\n    echo \"[SKIP] GLA already has results: $GLA_RESULT\"\n    # Verify it's valid (acc > 0.1 on lambada)\n    python3 -c \"\nimport json\nwith open('$GLA_RESULT') as f:\n    d = json.load(f)\nacc = d['results']['lambada_openai']['acc,none']\nprint(f'GLA lambada acc={acc:.4f}')\nassert acc > 0.1, 'Invalid GLA results!'\nprint('GLA results VALID')\n\"\nelse\n    eval_model \"GLA\" \"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-GLA-pure-baseline\"\nfi\n\neval_model \"DeltaNet\" \"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-DeltaNet-pure\"\neval_model \"HGRN\" \"$BASEDIR/models/340M-20B-HGRN-pure-baseline\"\n\necho \"==========================================\"\necho \"ALL PURE BASELINE EVALS COMPLETE\"\necho \"==========================================\"\nSCRIPT\nchmod +x /root/entrainment_gated_ft/results/exp005_zoo_expansion/run_pure_v10.sh"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":437,"cache_read_input_tokens":88361,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":437},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"f91c7e3f-c021-4ec0-8958-c2b588a62e76","uuid":"5f47024e-e221-4c0f-883b-da7b08ef3ae1"}
